            if ![n, u, lo, hi].iter().all(|&v| nonnegative(v)) {
                return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_STATE_DOMAIN"));
            }
            if n == 0.0 {
                if u != 0.0 || lo != 0.0 || hi != 0.0 {
                    return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_EMPTY_SUPPORT"));
                }
            } else {
                let i = k % self.n_energy();
                let mean = support_mean(lo, hi, n, u);
                if lo < self.edges[i]
                    || hi > self.edges[i + 1]
                    || lo > hi
                    || !mean.is_finite()
                    || (lo == hi && u != lo * n)
                    || (lo != hi && (u < lo * n || u > hi * n || mean < lo || mean > hi))
                {
                    return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_SUPPORT_DOMAIN"));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn ray_geometry(&self, now: &GeometrySnapshot) -> Result<Vec<RayGeometry>, ForwardError> {
        now.volume_factor()?;
        let isotropic = self
            .reference
            .scale_factors
            .windows(2)
            .all(|x| x[0] == x[1])
            && now.scale_factors.windows(2).all(|x| x[0] == x[1]);
        let mut result = Vec::with_capacity(self.source.angular_cells.len());
        for a in &self.source.angular_cells {
            let (scale, mu, jac) = if isotropic {
                (
                    self.reference.scale_factors[0] / now.scale_factors[0],
                    a.mu,
                    1.0,
                )
            } else {
                let ray =
                    CharacteristicRay::new(1.0, [(1.0 - a.mu * a.mu).sqrt(), 0.0, a.mu], 0.0)?;
                let moved = ray.pullback(&self.reference, now)?;
                (
                    moved.energy_ev,
                    moved.direction[2],
                    ray.solid_angle_jacobian(&self.reference, now)?,
                )
            };
            if !scale.is_finite() || scale <= 0.0 || !nonnegative(a.count_fraction * jac) {
                return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_GEOMETRY_DOMAIN"));
            }
            result.push(RayGeometry {
                scale,
                mu,
                angular_weight: a.count_fraction * jac,
            });
        }
        let norm = sum(result.iter().map(|g| g.angular_weight));
        if !norm.is_finite() || norm <= 0.0 {
            return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_ANGLE_DOMAIN"));
        }
        for g in &mut result {
            g.angular_weight /= norm;
        }
        Ok(result)
    }
    pub fn inventory(
        &self,
        state: &PhotonState,
        now: &GeometrySnapshot,
    ) -> Result<(f64, f64), ForwardError> {
        self.validate_state(state)?;
        let rays = self.ray_geometry(now)?;
        let n = sum(state.number.iter().copied());
        let u = sum(state
            .energy_ref
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(k, &u)| u * rays[k / self.n_energy()].scale));
        if !nonnegative(n) || !nonnegative(u) {
            return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_INVENTORY"));
        }
        Ok((n, u))
    }
    /// Test/point-source helper: a true delta keeps equal physical support.
    pub fn add_delta(
        &self,
        state: &mut PhotonState,
        ray: usize,
        q: f64,
        number: f64,
    ) -> Result<(), ForwardError> {
        self.validate_state(state)?;
        if ray >= self.source.angular_cells.len()
            || !nonnegative(number)
            || !q.is_finite()
            || q < self.edges[0]
            || q > *self.edges.last().unwrap()
        {
            return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_DELTA_DOMAIN"));
        }
        if number == 0.0 {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let i = self
            .edges
            .partition_point(|&e| e <= q)
            .saturating_sub(1)
            .min(self.n_energy() - 1);
        let k = ray * self.n_energy() + i;
        let mut next = state.clone();
        add_support(&mut next, k, number, q * number, q, q);
        self.validate_state(&next)?;
        *state = next;
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn inject(
        &self,
        state: &mut PhotonState,
        now: &GeometrySnapshot,
        zbar: f64,
        dt_s: f64,
    ) -> Result<SourceLedger, ForwardError> {
        self.validate_state(state)?;
        if !nonnegative(dt_s) {
            return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_SOURCE_DT"));
        }
        let amount = self.source.at_redshift(zbar)?.per_h_s * dt_s;
        if !nonnegative(amount) {
            return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_SOURCE_AMOUNT"));
        }
        let rays = self.ray_geometry(now)?;
        // Check full emission support before any mutation or partial deposition.
        for ray in &rays {
            for c in &self.source.spectral_cells {
                if c.lower_ev / ray.scale < self.edges[0]
                    || c.upper_ev / ray.scale > *self.edges.last().unwrap()
                {
                    return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_SOURCE_OUTSIDE_GRID"));
                }
            }
        }
        let mut next = state.clone();
        let mut ledger = SourceLedger::default();
        for (j, ray) in rays.iter().enumerate() {
            for (s, c) in self.source.spectral_cells.iter().enumerate() {
                let amp = amount * ray.angular_weight * c.photon_fraction / self.source_norms[s];
                if amp == 0.0 {
                    continue;
                }
                for i in 0..self.n_energy() {
                    let lo = c.lower_ev.max(self.edges[i] * ray.scale);
                    let hi = c.upper_ev.min(self.edges[i + 1] * ray.scale);
                    if hi <= lo {
                        continue;
                    }
                    let (n, u) = source_moments(lo, hi);
                    let n = amp * n;
                    let u = amp * u;
                    // Grid-edge roundoff must not expand the cell's support.
                    let qlo = if lo == self.edges[i] * ray.scale {
                        self.edges[i]
                    } else {
                        lo / ray.scale
                    };
                    let qhi = if hi == self.edges[i + 1] * ray.scale {
                        self.edges[i + 1]
                    } else {
                        hi / ray.scale
                    };
                    add_support(
                        &mut next,
                        j * self.n_energy() + i,
                        n,
                        u / ray.scale,
                        qlo,
                        qhi,
                    );
                    ledger.added_photons_per_h += n;
                    ledger.added_energy_ev_per_h += u;
                }
            }
        }
        self.validate_state(&next)?;
        *state = next;
        Ok(ledger)
    }
    pub fn prepare(
        &self,
        state: &PhotonState,
        now: &GeometrySnapshot,
    ) -> Result<PreparedAbsorption, ForwardError> {
        if self.prepare_pool.width() == 1 {
            return self.prepare_legacy_serial(state, now);
        }
        self.validate_state(state)?;
        let rays = Arc::new(self.ray_geometry(now)?);
        let snapshot = Arc::new(state.clone());
        let work_state = Arc::clone(&snapshot);
        let n_energy = self.n_energy();
        let cells = self.prepare_pool.map_ordered(self.cell_count(), move |k| {
            prepare_cell(k, &work_state, rays[k / n_energy].scale)
        })?;
        let mut nodes = Vec::new();
        let mut diagnostics = QuadratureDiagnostics::default();
        // Original cell order and each cell's original node order. Each tail
        // diagnostic has exactly one += per cell in cell_quadrature; replaying
        // these individual terms preserves the original addition association.
        for cell in cells {
            diagnostics.max_count_relative_defect = diagnostics
                .max_count_relative_defect
                .max(cell.diagnostics.max_count_relative_defect);
            diagnostics.max_energy_relative_defect = diagnostics
                .max_energy_relative_defect
                .max(cell.diagnostics.max_energy_relative_defect);
            diagnostics.max_asymptotic_mean_defect_ev = diagnostics
                .max_asymptotic_mean_defect_ev
                .max(cell.diagnostics.max_asymptotic_mean_defect_ev);
            diagnostics.omitted_tail_number_bound_per_h +=
                cell.diagnostics.omitted_tail_number_bound_per_h;
            diagnostics.omitted_tail_energy_bound_ev_per_h +=
                cell.diagnostics.omitted_tail_energy_bound_ev_per_h;
            diagnostics.omitted_tail_endpoint_moment_bound_ev_per_h +=
                cell.diagnostics.omitted_tail_endpoint_moment_bound_ev_per_h;
            nodes.extend(cell.nodes);
        }
        Ok(PreparedAbsorption {
            template: Arc::unwrap_or_clone(snapshot),
            nodes,
            diagnostics,
        })
    }
    pub fn prepare_legacy_serial(
        &self,
        state: &PhotonState,
        now: &GeometrySnapshot,
    ) -> Result<PreparedAbsorption, ForwardError> {
        self.validate_state(state)?;
        let rays = self.ray_geometry(now)?;
        let mut prepared = PreparedAbsorption {
            template: state.clone(),
            nodes: Vec::new(),
            diagnostics: QuadratureDiagnostics::default(),
        };
        let provider = AtomicProvider::reference();
        let species = [Absorber::HI, Absorber::HeI, Absorber::HeII];
        let model = HHeModel::controlled_fixture();
        for k in 0..self.cell_count() {
            let n = state.number[k];
            if n == 0.0 {
                continue;
            }
            let r = rays[k / self.n_energy()].scale;
            let weights = cell_quadrature(
                state.support_lo[k],
                state.support_hi[k],
                n,
                state.energy_ref[k],
                r,
                &mut prepared.diagnostics,
            )?;
            for (q, number) in weights {
                let e = q * r;
                let mut sigma = [0.0; 3];
                let mut excess = [0.0; 3];
                for a in 0..3 {
                    sigma[a] = provider.cross_section(species[a], e)?;
                    if sigma[a] > 0.0 {
                        excess[a] = e - model.threshold_ev[a];
                    }
                }
                prepared.nodes.push(AbsorptionNode {
                    cell: k,
                    q,
                    number,
                    sigma,
                    excess_ev: excess,
                });
            }
        }
        Ok(prepared)
    }
    pub fn evaluate(
        &self,
        prepared: &PreparedAbsorption,
        absorber_cm3: [f64; 3],
        n_h_cm3: f64,
        dt_s: f64,
    ) -> Result<AbsorptionResult, ForwardError> {
        if prepared.template.number.len() != self.cell_count() {
            return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_CACHE_SHAPE"));
        }
        prepared.evaluate(absorber_cm3, n_h_cm3, dt_s)
    }
}
struct PreparedCell {
    nodes: Vec<AbsorptionNode>,
    diagnostics: QuadratureDiagnostics,
}
fn prepare_cell(k: usize, state: &PhotonState, r: f64) -> Result<PreparedCell, ForwardError> {
    let mut diagnostics = QuadratureDiagnostics::default();
    let n = state.number[k];
    if n == 0.0 {
        return Ok(PreparedCell {
            nodes: Vec::new(),
            diagnostics,
        });
    }
    let weights = cell_quadrature(
        state.support_lo[k],
        state.support_hi[k],
        n,
        state.energy_ref[k],
        r,
        &mut diagnostics,
    )?;
    let provider = AtomicProvider::reference();
    let species = [Absorber::HI, Absorber::HeI, Absorber::HeII];
    let model = HHeModel::controlled_fixture();
    let mut nodes = Vec::with_capacity(weights.len());
    for (q, number) in weights {
        let e = q * r;
        let mut sigma = [0.0; 3];
        let mut excess = [0.0; 3];
        for a in 0..3 {
            sigma[a] = provider.cross_section(species[a], e)?;
            if sigma[a] > 0.0 {
                excess[a] = e - model.threshold_ev[a];
            }
        }
        nodes.push(AbsorptionNode {
            cell: k,
            q,
            number,
            sigma,
            excess_ev: excess,
        });
    }
    Ok(PreparedCell { nodes, diagnostics })
}

fn add_support(s: &mut PhotonState, k: usize, n: f64, u: f64, lo: f64, hi: f64) {
    if n == 0.0 {
        return;
    }
    if s.number[k] == 0.0 {
        s.support_lo[k] = lo;
        s.support_hi[k] = hi;
    } else {
        s.support_lo[k] = s.support_lo[k].min(lo);
        s.support_hi[k] = s.support_hi[k].max(hi);
    }
    s.number[k] += n;
    s.energy_ref[k] += u;
}

/// Positive quadrature of the bounded exponential, followed by a positive
/// discrete exponential tilt that reproduces the input number and energy.
fn cell_quadrature(
    lo: f64,
    hi: f64,
    n: f64,
    u: f64,
    r: f64,
    diagnostics: &mut QuadratureDiagnostics,
) -> Result<Vec<(f64, f64)>, ForwardError> {
    let mean = support_mean(lo, hi, n, u);
    if lo == hi {
        if u != lo * n {
            return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_DELTA_MOMENT"));
        }
        return Ok(vec![(lo, n)]);
    }
    if mean == lo || mean == hi {
        if mean < lo || mean > hi {
            return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_DELTA_MOMENT"));
        }
        return Ok(vec![(mean, n)]);
    }
    let width = hi - lo;
    let gap = (mean - lo).min(hi - mean);
    let beta = match positive_reconstruct(lo, hi, mean) {
        Ok(fit) => fit.beta,
        Err(_) => {
            // For |beta|>30, coth(beta)-1 differs from one by <1.76e-26.
            let b = width / (2.0 * gap);
            if !b.is_finite() || b <= 30.0 {
                return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_RECONSTRUCTION_DOMAIN"));
            }
            diagnostics.max_asymptotic_mean_defect_ev = diagnostics
                .max_asymptotic_mean_defect_ev
                .max(width * (-2.0 * b).exp() / -(-2.0 * b).exp_m1());
            if mean < 0.5 * (lo + hi) {
                -b
            } else {
                b
            }
        }
    };
    let steep = beta.abs();
    let mut points = Vec::new();
    points.push(lo);
    for cut in FIT_THRESHOLDS_EV {
        let q = cut / r;
        if q > lo && q < hi {
            points.push(q);
        }
    }
    points.push(hi);
    points.sort_by(f64::total_cmp);
    let mut base = Vec::new();
    if steep <= 30.0 {
        for pair in points.windows(2) {
            let panels = ((pair[1] - pair[0]) / width * 2.0 * steep / 3.0)
                .ceil()
                .max(1.0) as usize;
            for p in 0..panels {
                let a = pair[0] + (pair[1] - pair[0]) * p as f64 / panels as f64;
                let b = pair[0] + (pair[1] - pair[0]) * (p + 1) as f64 / panels as f64;
                let half = (b - a) * 0.5;
                for i in 0..8 {
                    let q = (a + b) * 0.5 + half * X8[i];
                    let x = 2.0 * (q - lo) / width - 1.0;
                    let weight = W8[i] * half / width * (beta * x - steep).exp();
                    if weight > 0.0 {
                        base.push((q, weight));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        let endpoint = if beta > 0.0 { hi } else { lo };
        let distance = width / (2.0 * steep);
        let ymax = (2.0 * steep).min(48.0);
        let mut ypoints = vec![0.0, ymax];
        for q in points {
            let y = (endpoint - q) * beta.signum() / distance;
            if y > 0.0 && y < ymax {
                ypoints.push(y);
            }
        }
        for p in 1..16 {
            let y = 3.0 * p as f64;
            if y < ymax {
                ypoints.push(y);
            }
        }
        ypoints.sort_by(f64::total_cmp);
        for pair in ypoints.windows(2) {
            let half = 0.5 * (pair[1] - pair[0]);
            for i in 0..8 {
                let y = 0.5 * (pair[0] + pair[1]) + half * X8[i];
                let q = endpoint - beta.signum() * distance * y;
                let weight = W8[i] * half * (-y).exp();
                if weight > 0.0 {
                    base.push((q, weight));
                }
            }
        }
        let tail = (-48.0_f64).exp() / -(-2.0 * steep).exp_m1();
        diagnostics.omitted_tail_number_bound_per_h += n * tail;
        diagnostics.omitted_tail_energy_bound_ev_per_h += n * hi * tail;
        diagnostics.omitted_tail_endpoint_moment_bound_ev_per_h += n * distance * 49.0 * tail;
    }
    if base.len() > MAX_NODES_PER_CELL || base.is_empty() {
        return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_QUADRATURE_CAP"));
    }
    let node_lo = base.iter().map(|x| x.0).fold(f64::INFINITY, f64::min);
    let node_hi = base.iter().map(|x| x.0).fold(f64::NEG_INFINITY, f64::max);
    if mean < node_lo || mean > node_hi {
        return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_QUADRATURE_CAP"));
    }
    let scale = gap;
    let eval = |lambda: f64| -> (Vec<f64>, f64, f64) {
        let logs: Vec<_> = base
            .iter()
            .map(|&(q, w)| w.ln() + lambda * (q - mean) / scale)
            .collect();
        let max = logs.iter().copied().fold(f64::NEG_INFINITY, f64::max);
        let weights: Vec<_> = logs.iter().map(|l| (l - max).exp()).collect();
        let total = sum(weights.iter().copied());
        let m = sum(base
            .iter()
            .zip(&weights)
            .map(|(&(q, _), w)| w * ((q - mean) / scale)))
            / total;
        let second = sum(base
            .iter()
            .zip(&weights)
            .map(|(&(q, _), w)| w * ((q - mean) / scale).powi(2)))
            / total;
        (weights, m, (second - m * m).max(0.0))
    };
    let (mut lower, mut upper) = (-1.0, 1.0);
    for _ in 0..32 {
        if eval(lower).1 <= 0.0 {
            break;
        }
        lower *= 2.0;
    }
    for _ in 0..32 {
        if eval(upper).1 >= 0.0 {
            break;
        }
        upper *= 2.0;
    }
    if eval(lower).1 > 0.0 || eval(upper).1 < 0.0 {
        return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_TILT_BRACKET"));
    }
    let mut lambda = 0.0;
    for _ in 0..80 {
        let (_, m, variance) = eval(lambda);
        if m.abs() < 8.0 * f64::EPSILON {
            break;
        }
        if m < 0.0 {
            lower = lambda;
        } else {
            upper = lambda;
        }
        let candidate = lambda - m / variance;
        lambda = if candidate.is_finite() && candidate > lower && candidate < upper {
            candidate
        } else {
            0.5 * (lower + upper)
        };
    }
    let (weights, _, _) = eval(lambda);
    let norm = sum(weights.iter().copied());
    let result: Vec<_> = base
        .iter()
        .zip(weights)
        .map(|(&(q, _), w)| (q, n * (w / norm)))
        .collect();
    let count_defect = (sum(result.iter().map(|x| x.1)) - n).abs();
    let energy_defect = (sum(result.iter().map(|x| x.0 * x.1)) - u).abs();
    let energy_scale = u.abs().max(n * width);
    if count_defect > 64.0 * f64::EPSILON * n || energy_defect > 128.0 * f64::EPSILON * energy_scale
    {
        return Err(bad("AXBI_COVARIANT_MOMENT_FIT"));
    }
    diagnostics.max_count_relative_defect =
        diagnostics.max_count_relative_defect.max(count_defect / n);
    diagnostics.max_energy_relative_defect = diagnostics
        .max_energy_relative_defect
        .max(energy_defect / energy_scale);
    Ok(result)
}

/// Diagnostic passive-compartment partition. Production callers are unchanged.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct HiPartition {
    pub active: PhotonState,
    pub inactive_number: Vec<f64>,
    pub inactive_energy_ref: Vec<f64>,
    pub diagnostics: QuadratureDiagnostics,
    pub quadrature_nodes: usize,
}
impl CovariantTransport {
    /// Pure restriction at actual HI fit energy. Below-HI photons retain their
    /// original reference cell and both moments; threshold equality is active.
    pub fn partition_below_hi(
        &self,
        state: &PhotonState,
        now: &GeometrySnapshot,
    ) -> Result<HiPartition, ForwardError> {
        self.validate_state(state)?;
        let rays = self.ray_geometry(now)?;
        let mut out = HiPartition {
            active: self.empty_state(),
            inactive_number: vec![0.0; self.cell_count()],
            inactive_energy_ref: vec![0.0; self.cell_count()],
            diagnostics: QuadratureDiagnostics::default(),
            quadrature_nodes: 0,
        };
        for k in 0..self.cell_count() {
            let (n, u, lo, hi) = (
                state.number[k],
                state.energy_ref[k],
                state.support_lo[k],
                state.support_hi[k],
            );
            if n == 0.0 {
                continue;
            }
            let r = rays[k / self.n_energy()].scale;
            if hi * r < FIT_THRESHOLDS_EV[0] {
                out.inactive_number[k] = n;
                out.inactive_energy_ref[k] = u;
                continue;
            }
            if lo * r >= FIT_THRESHOLDS_EV[0] {
                out.active.number[k] = n;
                out.active.energy_ref[k] = u;
                out.active.support_lo[k] = lo;
                out.active.support_hi[k] = hi;
                continue;
            }
            let cut = FIT_THRESHOLDS_EV[0] / r;
            let nodes = cell_quadrature(lo, hi, n, u, r, &mut out.diagnostics)?;
            out.quadrature_nodes += nodes.len();
            let active: Vec<_> = nodes
                .iter()
                .copied()
                .filter(|&(q, _)| q * r >= FIT_THRESHOLDS_EV[0])
                .collect();
            let passive: Vec<_> = nodes
                .iter()
                .copied()
                .filter(|&(q, _)| q * r < FIT_THRESHOLDS_EV[0])
                .collect();
            let (an, au) = restricted_moments(&active, lo.max(cut), hi);
            let (pn, pu) = restricted_moments(&passive, lo, hi.min(cut));
            let nd = (an + pn - n).abs();
            let uscale = u.abs().max(n * (hi - lo));
            let ud = (au + pu - u).abs();
            if nd > 64.0 * f64::EPSILON * n || ud > 128.0 * f64::EPSILON * uscale {
                return Err(bad("AXBI_HI_PARTITION_MOMENT_DEFECT"));
            }
            if an > 0.0 {
                out.active.number[k] = an;
                out.active.energy_ref[k] = au;
                out.active.support_lo[k] = lo.max(cut);
                out.active.support_hi[k] = hi;
            }
            if !nonnegative(pn) || !nonnegative(pu) || pu < lo * pn || pu > hi.min(cut) * pn {
                return Err(bad("AXBI_HI_PARTITION_PASSIVE_SUPPORT"));
            }
            out.inactive_number[k] = pn;
            out.inactive_energy_ref[k] = pu;
        }
        self.validate_state(&out.active)?;
        Ok(out)
    }
}
fn restricted_moments(nodes: &[(f64, f64)], lo: f64, hi: f64) -> (f64, f64) {
    let n = sum(nodes.iter().map(|&(_, n)| n));
    if n == 0.0 {
        return (0.0, 0.0);
    }
    let lower = sum(nodes.iter().map(|&(q, n)| (q - lo) * n));
    let upper = sum(nodes.iter().map(|&(q, n)| (hi - q) * n));
    let u = if lower <= upper {
        lo.mul_add(n, lower)
    } else {
        hi.mul_add(n, -upper)
    };
    (n, u)
}

impl CovariantTransport {
    /// Transactional repeated passive-pool addition in fixed reference cells.
    /// Centered moments preserve endpoint realizability without clipping.
    pub fn merge_inactive(
        &self,
        number: &mut Vec<f64>,
        energy_ref: &mut Vec<f64>,
        added_number: &[f64],
        added_energy_ref: &[f64],
    ) -> Result<(), ForwardError> {
        if [
            number.len(),
            energy_ref.len(),
            added_number.len(),
            added_energy_ref.len(),
        ]
        .iter()
        .any(|&n| n != self.cell_count())
        {
            return Err(bad("AXBI_INACTIVE_MERGE_SHAPE"));
        }
        let mut next_n = vec![0.0; self.cell_count()];
        let mut next_u = vec![0.0; self.cell_count()];
        for k in 0..self.cell_count() {
            let lo = self.edges[k % self.n_energy()];
            let hi = self.edges[k % self.n_energy() + 1];
            let (n0, u0, n1, u1) = (
                number[k],
                energy_ref[k],
                added_number[k],
                added_energy_ref[k],
            );
            if ![n0, u0, n1, u1].iter().all(|&v| nonnegative(v))
                || u0 < lo * n0
                || u0 > hi * n0
                || u1 < lo * n1
                || u1 > hi * n1
            {
                return Err(bad("AXBI_INACTIVE_MERGE_SUPPORT"));
            }
            if n1 == 0.0 {
                next_n[k] = n0;
                next_u[k] = u0;
                continue;
            }
            if n0 == 0.0 {
                next_n[k] = n1;
                next_u[k] = u1;
                continue;
            }
            let n = n0 + n1;
            let lower = (u0 - lo * n0) + (u1 - lo * n1);
            let upper = (hi * n0 - u0) + (hi * n1 - u1);
            let u = if lower <= upper {
                lo.mul_add(n, lower)
            } else {
                hi.mul_add(n, -upper)
            };
            if !nonnegative(n) || !nonnegative(u) || u < lo * n || u > hi * n {
                return Err(bad("AXBI_INACTIVE_MERGE_REALIZABILITY"));
            }
            let scale = (u0 + u1).abs().max(n * (hi - lo));
            if (u - (u0 + u1)).abs() > 128.0 * f64::EPSILON * scale {
                return Err(bad("AXBI_INACTIVE_MERGE_MOMENT_DEFECT"));
            }
            next_n[k] = n;
            next_u[k] = u;
        }
        *number = next_n;
        *energy_ref = next_u;
        Ok(())
    }
}

AXBI_SOURCE_BOUNDARY
//! Bounded persistent workers with indexed, caller-ordered results.
//! Scheduling never performs a floating-point reduction.
use crate::ForwardError;
use std::fmt;
use std::panic::{catch_unwind, AssertUnwindSafe};
use std::sync::{mpsc, Arc, Mutex};
use std::thread::{self, JoinHandle};

type Job = Box<dyn FnOnce() + Send + 'static>;
fn bad(tag: &'static str) -> ForwardError {
    ForwardError::InvalidInput(tag)
}

pub struct OrderedWorkerPool {
    width: usize,
    sender: Option<mpsc::SyncSender<Job>>,
    workers: Vec<JoinHandle<()>>,
}
impl fmt::Debug for OrderedWorkerPool {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("OrderedWorkerPool")
            .field("width", &self.width)
            .finish()
    }
}
impl OrderedWorkerPool {
    pub fn new(width: usize) -> Result<Self, ForwardError> {
        if !(1..=64).contains(&width) {
            return Err(bad("AXBI_TRANSPORT_THREADS"));
        }
        if width == 1 {
            return Ok(Self {
                width,
                sender: None,
                workers: Vec::new(),
            });
        }
        let (sender, receiver) = mpsc::sync_channel::<Job>(2 * width);
        let receiver = Arc::new(Mutex::new(receiver));
        let mut workers = Vec::with_capacity(width);
        for index in 0..width {
            let receiver = Arc::clone(&receiver);
            match thread::Builder::new()
                .name(format!("rei-cell-{index}"))
                .spawn(move || loop {
                    let message = match receiver.lock() {
                        Ok(r) => r.recv(),
                        Err(_) => return,
                    };
                    match message {
                        Ok(job) => job(),
                        Err(_) => return,
                    }
                }) {
                Ok(worker) => workers.push(worker),
                Err(_) => {
                    drop(sender);
                    for worker in workers {
                        let _ = worker.join();
                    }
                    return Err(bad("AXBI_WORKER_SPAWN"));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(Self {
            width,
            sender: Some(sender),
            workers,
        })
    }
    pub fn width(&self) -> usize {
        self.width
    }
    /// Dynamic contiguous chunks, finite job/result channels. All launched work
    /// is collected before the lowest-index cell error is returned.
    pub fn map_ordered<T, F>(&self, count: usize, function: F) -> Result<Vec<T>, ForwardError>
    where
        T: Send + 'static,
        F: Fn(usize) -> Result<T, ForwardError> + Send + Sync + 'static,
    {
        if self.width == 1 {
            return (0..count)
                .map(|index| {
                    catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| function(index)))
                        .unwrap_or_else(|_| Err(bad("AXBI_WORKER_TASK_PANIC")))
                })
                .collect();
        }
        if count == 0 {
            return Ok(Vec::new());
        }
        let stride = count.div_ceil(4 * self.width).max(1);
        let jobs = count.div_ceil(stride);
        let (reply, received) = mpsc::sync_channel(jobs);
        let function = Arc::new(function);
        for start in (0..count).step_by(stride) {
            let end = (start + stride).min(count);
            let work = Arc::clone(&function);
            let reply = reply.clone();
            self.sender
                .as_ref()
                .ok_or_else(|| bad("AXBI_WORKER_CLOSED"))?
                .send(Box::new(move || {
                    let values = (start..end)
                        .map(|index| {
                            catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| work(index)))
                                .unwrap_or_else(|_| Err(bad("AXBI_WORKER_TASK_PANIC")))
                        })
                        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
                    drop(work);
                    let _ = reply.send((start, values));
                }))
                .map_err(|_| bad("AXBI_WORKER_CLOSED"))?;
        }
        drop(reply);
        drop(function);
        let mut slots = (0..count)
            .map(|_| None)
            .collect::<Vec<Option<Result<T, ForwardError>>>>();
        for _ in 0..jobs {
            let (start, values) = received.recv().map_err(|_| bad("AXBI_WORKER_RESULT"))?;
            for (offset, value) in values.into_iter().enumerate() {
                slots[start + offset] = Some(value);
            }
        }
        slots
            .into_iter()
            .map(|slot| slot.ok_or_else(|| bad("AXBI_WORKER_RESULT"))?)
            .collect()
    }
}
impl Drop for OrderedWorkerPool {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        drop(self.sender.take());
        for worker in self.workers.drain(..) {
            let _ = worker.join();
        }
    }
}

AXBI_SOURCE_BOUNDARY
//! Positive two-moment bounded-exponential photon spectrum reconstruction.
//! C6 scientific repair: split H/He photoelectric thresholds before integrating
//! opacity and gas heating. This is a closure, not the unique true spectrum.
use crate::{Absorber, AtomicProvider, ForwardError, HHeModel};

const X8: [f64; 8] = [
    -0.9602898564975363,
    -0.7966664774136267,
    -0.5255324099163290,
    -0.1834346424956498,
    0.1834346424956498,
    0.5255324099163290,
    0.7966664774136267,
    0.9602898564975363,
];
const W8: [f64; 8] = [
    0.1012285362903763,
    0.2223810344533745,
    0.3137066458778873,
    0.3626837833783619,
    0.3626837833783619,
    0.3137066458778873,
    0.2223810344533745,
    0.1012285362903763,
];
fn bad() -> ForwardError {
    ForwardError::InvalidInput("AXBI_Q_EXPONENTIAL_CLOSURE_DOMAIN")
}
fn valid(v: f64) -> bool {
    v.is_finite() && v >= 0.0
}
fn mean_x(beta: f64) -> f64 {
    let b = beta.abs();
    if b < 1e-3 {
        let b2 = beta * beta;
        beta * (1.0 / 3.0 - b2 / 45.0 + 2.0 * b2 * b2 / 945.0 - b2 * b2 * b2 / 4725.0)
    } else {
        beta.signum() * (1.0 + 2.0 / (2.0 * b).exp_m1() - 1.0 / b)
    }
}
fn variance_x(beta: f64) -> f64 {
    let b = beta.abs();
    if b < 1e-3 {
        let b2 = b * b;
        1.0 / 3.0 - b2 / 15.0 + 2.0 * b2 * b2 / 189.0
    } else if b > 30.0 {
        1.0 / (b * b)
    } else {
        1.0 / (b * b) - 1.0 / b.sinh().powi(2)
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct PositiveReconstruction {
    pub lower_ev: f64,
    pub upper_ev: f64,
    pub beta: f64,
    pub normalizer: f64,
}
impl PositiveReconstruction {
    pub fn mean_ev(&self) -> f64 {
        (self.lower_ev + self.upper_ev) * 0.5
            + (self.upper_ev - self.lower_ev) * 0.5 * mean_x(self.beta)
    }
}
pub fn positive_reconstruct(
    lo: f64,
    hi: f64,
    mean: f64,
) -> Result<PositiveReconstruction, ForwardError> {
    if !(lo.is_finite()
        && hi.is_finite()
        && mean.is_finite()
        && hi > lo
        && lo > 0.0
        && mean > lo
        && mean < hi)
    {
        return Err(bad());
    }
    let eta = (2.0 * mean - lo - hi) / (hi - lo);
    if eta.abs() >= 1.0 {
        return Err(bad());
    }
    let beta = if eta == 0.0 {
        0.0
    } else {
        let mut left = 0.0;
        let mut right = (2.0 / (1.0 - eta.abs())).max(2.0);
        if right > 25000.0 {
            return Err(bad());
        }
        let mut guess = if eta.abs() < 0.4 {
            3.0 * eta.abs()
        } else {
            1.0 / (1.0 - eta.abs())
        };
        guess = guess.clamp(left, right);
        for _ in 0..64 {
            let m = mean_x(guess);
            if m < eta.abs() {
                left = guess;
            } else {
                right = guess;
            }
            if (m - eta.abs()).abs() <= 2e-14 {
                break;
            }
            let deriv = variance_x(guess);
            let candidate = guess - (m - eta.abs()) / deriv;
            guess = if candidate.is_finite() && candidate > left && candidate < right {
                candidate
            } else {
                0.5 * (left + right)
            };
        }
        eta.signum() * guess
    };
    if (mean_x(beta) - eta).abs() > 2e-10 {
        return Err(bad());
    }
    let ab = beta.abs();
    let normalizer = if ab < 1e-5 {
        2.0 * (1.0 + beta * beta / 6.0 + beta.powi(4) / 120.0) * (-ab).exp()
    } else {
        -(-2.0 * ab).exp_m1() / ab
    };
    if !(normalizer.is_finite() && normalizer > 0.0) {
        return Err(bad());
    }
    Ok(PositiveReconstruction {
        lower_ev: lo,
        upper_ev: hi,
        beta,
        normalizer,
    })
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct PositiveBinAbsorption {
    pub surviving_photons_per_h: f64,
    pub surviving_energy_moment_ev_per_h: f64,
    pub absorbed_per_h: [f64; 3],
    pub binding_ev_per_h: f64,
    pub heat_ev_per_h: f64,
    pub quadrature_count_defect: f64,
    pub quadrature_mean_defect_ev: f64,
}
/// Frozen physical gas opacities, positive two-moment spectral shape in E_ref.
/// Threshold splitting occurs at each actual FIT threshold divided by R.
pub fn absorb_positive_bin(
    lo: f64,
    hi: f64,
    n: f64,
    uref: f64,
    r: f64,
    neutrals: [f64; 3],
    dt: f64,
) -> Result<PositiveBinAbsorption, ForwardError> {
    if !(valid(n)
        && n > 0.0
        && valid(uref)
        && r.is_finite()
        && r > 0.0
        && valid(dt)
        && neutrals.iter().all(|x| valid(*x)))
    {
        return Err(bad());
    }
    let mean = uref / n;
    let fit = positive_reconstruct(lo, hi, mean)?;
    let model = HHeModel::controlled_fixture();
    let provider = AtomicProvider::reference();
    let species = [Absorber::HI, Absorber::HeI, Absorber::HeII];
    let thresholds = [13.60, 24.59, 54.42];
    let mut points = [0.0; 5];
    points[0] = lo;
    let mut count = 1;
    for edge in thresholds {
        let cut = edge / r;
        if cut > lo && cut < hi {
            points[count] = cut;
            count += 1;
        }
    }
    points[count] = hi;
    count += 1;
    let mut out = PositiveBinAbsorption::default();
    let mid = (lo + hi) * 0.5;
    let half = (hi - lo) * 0.5;
    let mut total_weight = 0.0;
    let mut quadrature_uref = 0.0;
    let mut lost_n = 0.0;
    let mut lost_uref = 0.0;
    let steep = fit.beta.abs();
    for pair in points[..count].windows(2) {
        let (a, b) = (pair[0], pair[1]);
        // Keep each exponent change <=3 across a subpanel when beta is large.
        let panels = (((b - a) / (hi - lo) * steep / 3.0).ceil() as usize).max(1);
        if panels > 2048 {
            return Err(bad());
        }
        for panel in 0..panels {
            let l = a + (b - a) * (panel as f64) / (panels as f64);
            let h = a + (b - a) * ((panel + 1) as f64) / (panels as f64);
            let hm = (h - l) * 0.5;
            let center = (l + h) * 0.5;
            for idx in 0..8 {
                let eref = center + hm * X8[idx];
                let x = (eref - mid) / half;
                let fraction = (fit.beta * x - steep).exp() / fit.normalizer * W8[idx] * hm / half;
                let incoming = n * fraction;
                total_weight += fraction;
                quadrature_uref += fraction * eref;
                let e = eref * r;
                let mut rates = [0.0; 3];
                for j in 0..3 {
                    if neutrals[j] > 0.0 && e >= thresholds[j] {
                        rates[j] =
                            model.c_cm_s * neutrals[j] * provider.cross_section(species[j], e)?;
                    }
                }
                let lambda: f64 = rates.iter().sum();
                if lambda <= 0.0 {
                    continue;
                }
                let event = incoming * (-(-lambda * dt).exp_m1());
                lost_n += event;
                lost_uref += event * eref;
                for j in 0..3 {
                    let absorbed = event * rates[j] / lambda;
                    out.absorbed_per_h[j] += absorbed;
                    out.binding_ev_per_h += absorbed * model.threshold_ev[j];
                    out.heat_ev_per_h += absorbed * (e - model.threshold_ev[j]);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    out.quadrature_count_defect = total_weight - 1.0;
    out.quadrature_mean_defect_ev = quadrature_uref - mean;
    if total_weight.is_nan()
        || (total_weight - 1.0).abs() > 2e-9
        || (quadrature_uref - mean).abs() > 2e-9 * (hi - lo)
    {
        return Err(bad());
    }
    out.surviving_photons_per_h = n - lost_n;
    out.surviving_energy_moment_ev_per_h = uref - lost_uref;
    if !(valid(out.surviving_photons_per_h)
        && valid(out.surviving_energy_moment_ev_per_h)
        && out.surviving_energy_moment_ev_per_h <= hi * out.surviving_photons_per_h * (1.0 + 1e-9)
        && out.surviving_energy_moment_ev_per_h >= lo * out.surviving_photons_per_h * (1.0 - 1e-9))
    {
        return Err(bad());
    }
    Ok(out)
}

AXBI_SOURCE_BOUNDARY
//! Stress-free, density-fixed axisymmetric Bianchi I in elapsed proper seconds.
//! Parameters have no physical defaults; SAME_H is an explicit counterfactual.
use crate::{AxisymmetricPoint, ForwardError, GeometryBackground, GeometrySnapshot};

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

pub const DEFAULT_PANELS: usize = 4096;
fn invalid(tag: &'static str) -> ForwardError {
    ForwardError::InvalidInput(tag)
}
fn finite(x: f64) -> Result<f64, ForwardError> {
    if x.is_finite() {
        Ok(x)
    } else {
        Err(invalid("AxbiNonFinite"))
    }
}
fn positive(x: f64) -> Result<f64, ForwardError> {
    if finite(x)? > 0.0 {
        Ok(x)
    } else {
        Err(invalid("AxbiPositive"))
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Cosmology {
    pub h0_per_s: f64,
    pub omega_m: f64,
    pub omega_r: f64,
    pub omega_lambda: f64,
}
impl Cosmology {
    pub fn hubble_flrw(&self, a: f64) -> Result<f64, ForwardError> {
        positive(a)?;
        positive(self.h0_per_s)?;
        for density in [self.omega_m, self.omega_r, self.omega_lambda] {
            if finite(density)? < 0.0 {
                return Err(invalid("AxbiDensity"));
            }
        }
        positive(
            self.h0_per_s
                * positive(
                    self.omega_m / a.powi(3) + self.omega_r / a.powi(4) + self.omega_lambda,
                )?
                .sqrt(),
        )
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum BackgroundFamily {
    PhysicalDensityFixed,
    SameHDiagnostic,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct BackgroundPoint {
    pub a: f64,
    pub b: f64,
    pub time_s: f64,
    pub h_flrw_per_s: f64,
    pub h_per_s: f64,
    pub s_per_s: f64,
    /// Instantaneous s/H, distinct from the fixed signed q_ref.
    pub sigma: f64,
}
/// Local physical increments from the actual binary64 scale endpoints.
/// The GL4/GL8 differences are diagnostics, not certified error bounds.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct BackgroundIncrement {
    pub proper_time_s: f64,
    pub delta_b: f64,
    pub gl4_time_difference_s: f64,
    pub gl4_b_difference: f64,
}
impl BackgroundPoint {
    pub fn geometry(&self) -> Result<GeometrySnapshot, ForwardError> {
        AxisymmetricPoint::new(self.a, self.b, self.h_per_s, self.s_per_s)?.snapshot()
    }
    pub fn sigma_squared_per_s2(&self) -> f64 {
        3.0 * self.s_per_s * self.s_per_s
    }
}
/// Transient exact-function telemetry; resident keys are bounded by 32.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PointCacheTelemetry {
    pub calls: u64,
    pub hits: u64,
    pub misses: u64,
    pub actual_uncached: u64,
    pub evictions: u64,
    pub resident_keys: Vec<u64>,
}
#[derive(Debug)]
struct PointRing {
    slots: [Option<(u64, BackgroundPoint)>; 32],
    next: usize,
}
#[derive(Debug)]
struct PointCache {
    ring: Mutex<PointRing>,
    enabled: AtomicBool,
    calls: AtomicU64,
    hits: AtomicU64,
    misses: AtomicU64,
    actual_uncached: AtomicU64,
    evictions: AtomicU64,
}
impl PointCache {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            ring: Mutex::new(PointRing {
                slots: [None; 32],
                next: 0,
            }),
            enabled: AtomicBool::new(true),
            calls: AtomicU64::new(0),
            hits: AtomicU64::new(0),
            misses: AtomicU64::new(0),
            actual_uncached: AtomicU64::new(0),
            evictions: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct AxbiBackground {
    cosmology: Cosmology,
    a_ref: f64,
    a_end: f64,
    b_ref: f64,
    q_ref: f64,
    family: BackgroundFamily,
    panels: usize,
    point_cache: Arc<PointCache>,
}
impl AxbiBackground {
    pub fn new(
        cosmology: Cosmology,
        a_ref: f64,
        a_end: f64,
        b_ref: f64,
        q_ref: f64,
        family: BackgroundFamily,
        panels: usize,
    ) -> Result<Self, ForwardError> {
        positive(a_ref)?;
        positive(a_end)?;
        finite(b_ref)?;
        finite(q_ref)?;
        if a_end <= a_ref || !(2..=65536).contains(&panels) || panels % 2 != 0 {
            return Err(invalid("AxbiDomainOrPanels"));
        }
        cosmology.hubble_flrw(a_ref)?;
        cosmology.hubble_flrw(a_end)?;
        let result = Self {
            cosmology,
            a_ref,
            a_end,
            b_ref,
            q_ref,
            family,
            panels,
            point_cache: Arc::new(PointCache::new()),
        };
        result.point_at_scale(a_end)?;
        Ok(result)
    }
    pub fn q_ref(&self) -> f64 {
        self.q_ref
    }
    pub fn family(&self) -> BackgroundFamily {
        self.family
    }
    fn rates(&self, a: f64) -> Result<(f64, f64, f64), ForwardError> {
        let hf = self.cosmology.hubble_flrw(a)?;
        let s = finite(
            self.q_ref * self.cosmology.hubble_flrw(self.a_ref)? * (self.a_ref / a).powi(3),
        )?;
        let h = match self.family {
            BackgroundFamily::PhysicalDensityFixed => positive(hf.hypot(s))?,
            BackgroundFamily::SameHDiagnostic => hf,
        };
        Ok((hf, h, s))
    }
    /// Integrate 1/H and s/H in local log scale without subtracting accumulated
    /// clocks. Both endpoints must be ordered and inside this background.
    pub fn interval_increment(
        &self,
        a0: f64,
        a1: f64,
    ) -> Result<BackgroundIncrement, ForwardError> {
        positive(a0)?;
        positive(a1)?;
        if a0 < self.a_ref || a1 > self.a_end || a1 < a0 {
            return Err(invalid("AxbiIntervalDomain"));
        }
        if a0 == a1 {
            return Ok(BackgroundIncrement {
                proper_time_s: 0.0,
                delta_b: 0.0,
                gl4_time_difference_s: 0.0,
                gl4_b_difference: 0.0,
            });
        }
        let dx = finite(((a1 - a0) / a0).ln_1p())?;
        // Keep the quadrature local even for a full smooth background interval.
        let panels = (dx / 0.01).ceil().max(1.0);
        if panels > 65536.0 {
            return Err(invalid("AxbiIntervalPanels"));
        }
        let panels = panels as usize;
        // Positive nodes and weights; their reflected partners complete each rule.
        const GL4: [(f64, f64); 2] = [
            (0.3399810435848563, 0.6521451548625461),
            (0.8611363115940526, 0.3478548451374538),
        ];
        const GL8: [(f64, f64); 4] = [
            (0.1834346424956498, 0.3626837833783620),
            (0.5255324099163290, 0.3137066458778873),
            (0.7966664774136267, 0.2223810344533745),
            (0.9602898564975363, 0.1012285362903763),
        ];
        let integrate = |rule: &[(f64, f64)]| -> Result<(f64, f64), ForwardError> {
            let (mut clock, mut shear) = (0.0, 0.0);
            let half_width = dx / (2.0 * panels as f64);
            for panel in 0..panels {
                for &(node, weight) in rule {
                    for sign in [-1.0, 1.0] {
                        let fraction = (panel as f64 + (1.0 + sign * node) * 0.5) / panels as f64;
                        let a = a0 * (fraction * dx).exp();
                        let (_, h, s) = self.rates(a)?;
                        clock += weight * half_width / h;
                        shear += weight * half_width * s / h;
                    }
                }
            }
            Ok((positive(clock)?, finite(shear)?))
        };
        let (t8, b8) = integrate(&GL8)?;
        let (t4, b4) = integrate(&GL4)?;
        Ok(BackgroundIncrement {
            proper_time_s: t8,
            delta_b: b8,
            gl4_time_difference_s: finite(t4 - t8)?,
            gl4_b_difference: finite(b4 - b8)?,
        })
    }
    /// Toggle exact memoization on this immutable model and its clones.
    /// Disabled calls neither consult nor modify the populated ring.
    pub fn set_point_cache_enabled(&self, enabled: bool) {
        self.point_cache.enabled.store(enabled, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }
    pub fn point_cache_enabled(&self) -> bool {
        self.point_cache.enabled.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
    }
    pub fn point_cache_telemetry(&self) -> PointCacheTelemetry {
        let c = &self.point_cache;
        PointCacheTelemetry {
            calls: c.calls.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
            hits: c.hits.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
            misses: c.misses.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
            actual_uncached: c.actual_uncached.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
            evictions: c.evictions.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
            resident_keys: c
                .ring
                .lock()
                .map(|r| r.slots.iter().flatten().map(|v| v.0).collect())
                .unwrap_or_default(),
        }
    }
    /// Reset counters only; constructor warming and resident entries remain.
    pub fn reset_point_cache_telemetry(&self) {
        let c = &self.point_cache;
        for counter in [
            &c.calls,
            &c.hits,
            &c.misses,
            &c.actual_uncached,
            &c.evictions,
        ] {
            counter.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
        }
    }
    pub fn point_at_scale(&self, a: f64) -> Result<BackgroundPoint, ForwardError> {
        let c = &self.point_cache;
        c.calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        // Preserve original validation and error priority before any lookup.
        positive(a)?;
        if a < self.a_ref || a > self.a_end {
            return Err(invalid("AxbiScaleDomain"));
        }
        if !c.enabled.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
            return self.point_at_scale_uncached(a);
        }
        let key = a.to_bits();
        if let Ok(ring) = c.ring.lock() {
            if let Some((_, point)) = ring.slots.iter().flatten().find(|v| v.0 == key) {
                c.hits.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                return Ok(*point);
            }
        }
        c.misses.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        // Compute outside the lock. Only fully validated successes enter it.
        let point = self.point_at_scale_uncached(a)?;
        if let Ok(mut ring) = c.ring.lock() {
            // Concurrent equal-key misses may both compute, but occupy one slot.
            if !ring.slots.iter().flatten().any(|v| v.0 == key) {
                let index = ring.next;
                if ring.slots[index].is_some() {
                    c.evictions.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                }
                ring.slots[index] = Some((key, point));
                ring.next = (index + 1) % 32;
            }
        }
        Ok(point)
    }
    /// Original arithmetic reference; bypasses all ring reads and writes.
    pub fn point_at_scale_uncached(&self, a: f64) -> Result<BackgroundPoint, ForwardError> {
        self.point_cache
            .actual_uncached
            .fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        positive(a)?;
        if a < self.a_ref || a > self.a_end {
            return Err(invalid("AxbiScaleDomain"));
        }
        let span = (a / self.a_ref).ln();
        let dx = span / self.panels as f64;
        let (mut clock, mut anisotropy) = (0.0, 0.0);
        for i in 0..=self.panels {
            let ai = self.a_ref * (dx * i as f64).exp();
            let (_, h, s) = self.rates(ai)?;
            let w = if i == 0 || i == self.panels {
                1.0
            } else if i % 2 == 0 {
                2.0
            } else {
                4.0
            };
            clock += w / h;
            anisotropy += w * s / h;
        }
        let (hf, h, s) = self.rates(a)?;
        let point = BackgroundPoint {
            a,
            b: finite(self.b_ref + anisotropy * dx / 3.0)?,
            time_s: finite(clock * dx / 3.0)?,
            h_flrw_per_s: hf,
            h_per_s: h,
            s_per_s: s,
            sigma: finite(s / h)?,
        };
        point.geometry()?;
        Ok(point)
    }
    pub fn point_at_time(&self, time_s: f64) -> Result<BackgroundPoint, ForwardError> {
        finite(time_s)?;
        let end = self.point_at_scale(self.a_end)?;
        if time_s < 0.0 || time_s > end.time_s {
            return Err(invalid("AxbiTimeDomain"));
        }
        if time_s == 0.0 {
            return self.point_at_scale(self.a_ref);
        }
        if time_s == end.time_s {
            return Ok(end);
        }
        let (mut lo, mut hi) = (self.a_ref.ln(), self.a_end.ln());
        for _ in 0..64 {
            let mid = (lo + hi) * 0.5;
            if self.point_at_scale(mid.exp())?.time_s < time_s {
                lo = mid;
            } else {
                hi = mid;
            }
        }
        self.point_at_scale(((lo + hi) * 0.5).exp())
    }
}
impl GeometryBackground for AxbiBackground {
    fn snapshot(&self, t: f64) -> Result<GeometrySnapshot, ForwardError> {
        self.point_at_time(t)?.geometry()
    }
}

/// Actual omitted-stress history: SI pressure/energy, elapsed proper seconds.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct FeedbackSample {
    pub a: f64,
    pub time_s: f64,
    pub pressure_anisotropy_j_m3: f64,
    pub extra_energy_j_m3: f64,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct FeedbackDiagnostic {
    pub max_abs_delta_s_over_h: f64,
    pub max_extra_energy_over_background: f64,
    /// This is a sampled trapezoidal perturbative estimate, not a rigorous bound.
    pub endpoint_delta_s_per_s: f64,
}
/// No stress history is inferred from q. Include free-streaming CMB stress as
/// well as ionizing photons. G and c must be supplied in SI units.
pub fn omitted_feedback(
    background: &AxbiBackground,
    samples: &[FeedbackSample],
    g_m3_kg_s2: f64,
    c_m_s: f64,
) -> Result<FeedbackDiagnostic, ForwardError> {
    positive(g_m3_kg_s2)?;
    positive(c_m_s)?;
    if samples.len() < 2 {
        return Err(invalid("AxbiFeedbackCoverage"));
    }
    if samples[0].a != background.a_ref || samples[0].time_s != 0.0 {
        return Err(invalid("AxbiFeedbackOrigin"));
    }
    let k = positive(8.0 * std::f64::consts::PI * g_m3_kg_s2 / (3.0 * c_m_s * c_m_s))?;
    let mut integral = 0.0;
    let mut diagnostic = FeedbackDiagnostic {
        max_abs_delta_s_over_h: 0.0,
        max_extra_energy_over_background: 0.0,
        endpoint_delta_s_per_s: 0.0,
    };
    for (i, sample) in samples.iter().enumerate() {
        let point = background.point_at_scale(sample.a)?;
        finite(sample.time_s)?;
        finite(sample.pressure_anisotropy_j_m3)?;
        if finite(sample.extra_energy_j_m3)? < 0.0 {
            return Err(invalid("AxbiFeedbackEnergy"));
        }
        if (sample.time_s - point.time_s).abs() > 1e-7 * point.time_s.max(1.0) {
            return Err(invalid("AxbiFeedbackClock"));
        }
        if i > 0 {
            let prev = &samples[i - 1];
            if sample.a <= prev.a || sample.time_s <= prev.time_s {
                return Err(invalid("AxbiFeedbackOrder"));
            }
            integral = finite(
                integral
                    + 0.5
                        * k
                        * (sample.a.powi(3) * sample.pressure_anisotropy_j_m3
                            + prev.a.powi(3) * prev.pressure_anisotropy_j_m3)
                        * (sample.time_s - prev.time_s),
            )?;
        }
        let ds = finite(integral / sample.a.powi(3))?;
        diagnostic.max_abs_delta_s_over_h = diagnostic
            .max_abs_delta_s_over_h
            .max(finite(ds.abs() / point.h_per_s)?);
        // Total isotropic background including Lambda: H_FLRW²/k.
        diagnostic.max_extra_energy_over_background =
            diagnostic.max_extra_energy_over_background.max(finite(
                sample.extra_energy_j_m3 * k / point.h_flrw_per_s.powi(2),
            )?);
        diagnostic.endpoint_delta_s_per_s = ds;
    }
    Ok(diagnostic)
}

/// Free-streamed initially isotropic massless-radiation moments, SI units.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct IsotropicRadiationMoments {
    pub energy_j_m3: f64,
    pub pressure_anisotropy_j_m3: f64,
    pub signed_energy_correction_j_m3: f64,
    pub dipole_energy_j_m3: f64,
}
/// Integrate initial directions mu0, not the evolved local angular measure.
/// The full effective Omega_r fluid may be used as a conservative free-stream
/// envelope; this is a perturbative diagnostic rather than coupled evolution.
pub fn isotropic_initial_radiation_moments(
    a: f64,
    b: f64,
    a_ref: f64,
    b_ref: f64,
    epsilon_ref_j_m3: f64,
    panels: usize,
) -> Result<IsotropicRadiationMoments, ForwardError> {
    positive(a)?;
    positive(a_ref)?;
    finite(b)?;
    finite(b_ref)?;
    if finite(epsilon_ref_j_m3)? < 0.0 || !(2..=65536).contains(&panels) || panels % 2 != 0 {
        return Err(invalid("AxbiRadiationDomain"));
    }
    let db = finite(b - b_ref)?;
    let ep = positive((2.0 * db).exp())?;
    let ez = positive((-4.0 * db).exp())?;
    let mut monopole = 0.0;
    let mut quadrupole = 0.0;
    for i in 0..=panels {
        let mu = i as f64 / panels as f64;
        let r = positive(((1.0 - mu * mu) * ep + mu * mu * ez).sqrt())?;
        let local_mu = mu * ez.sqrt() / r;
        let w = if i == 0 || i == panels {
            1.0
        } else if i % 2 == 0 {
            2.0
        } else {
            4.0
        };
        monopole += w * r;
        quadrupole += w * r * (3.0 * local_mu * local_mu - 1.0) / 2.0;
    }
    let eps_iso = finite(epsilon_ref_j_m3 * (a_ref / a).powi(4))?;
    let norm = 3.0 * panels as f64;
    let energy = finite(eps_iso * monopole / norm)?;
    Ok(IsotropicRadiationMoments {
        energy_j_m3: energy,
        pressure_anisotropy_j_m3: finite(eps_iso * quadrupole / norm)?,
        signed_energy_correction_j_m3: finite(energy - eps_iso)?,
        dipole_energy_j_m3: 0.0,
    })
}

AXBI_SOURCE_BOUNDARY
//! Frozen conditional MD14 comoving emissivity and truncated stellar blackbody.
//!
//! Sources and scope are recorded in AX01 SCENARIO_LOCK. The analytic high-z
//! continuation and 50,000 K blackbody are explicit model choices, not empirical
//! calibration. This module injects photon numbers; it does not force Gamma,
//! add photoheat, absorb photons, or create recombination escape photons.
use crate::{ForwardError, HHeModel, MPC_CM};

pub const SOURCE_ID: &str = "MD14_Z002_FESC_BB50K_13P6_100_V1";
pub const ENERGY_SUPPORT_EV: [f64; 2] = [13.6, 100.0];
pub const REDSHIFT_SUPPORT: [f64; 2] = [4.0, 20.0];
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct SpectralCell {
    pub lower_ev: f64,
    pub upper_ev: f64,
    /// Cell energy moment divided by its photon-number moment, not midpoint E.
    pub energy_ev: f64,
    pub photon_fraction: f64,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct AngularCell {
    pub mu: f64,
    /// Gauss-Legendre weight/2; total source is already 4pi-integrated.
    pub count_fraction: f64,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct EmissionRate {
    pub comoving_photons_per_s_cmpc3: f64,
    pub proper_photons_cm3_s: f64,
    pub per_h_s: f64,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct AxbiSource {
    pub spectral_cells: Vec<SpectralCell>,
    pub angular_cells: Vec<AngularCell>,
    pub normalization_relative_residual: f64,
    pub omitted_blackbody_tail_photon_fraction: f64,
    n_h0_cm3: f64,
    escape_fraction: f64,
}
fn invalid() -> ForwardError {
    ForwardError::InvalidInput("AXBI_SOURCE_DOMAIN_OR_QUADRATURE")
}
fn positive(x: f64) -> Result<f64, ForwardError> {
    if x.is_normal() && x > 0.0 {
        Ok(x)
    } else {
        Err(invalid())
    }
}
fn shape(energy_ev: f64) -> f64 {
    let c = HHeModel::controlled_fixture();
    let kt = c.kb_erg_k / c.ev_erg * 50000.0;
    energy_ev * energy_ev / (energy_ev / kt).exp_m1()
}
fn simpson(lo: f64, hi: f64, n: usize, energy_moment: bool) -> f64 {
    let step = (hi - lo) / n as f64;
    let mut sum = 0.0;
    for i in 0..=n {
        let e = lo + step * i as f64;
        let f = shape(e) * if energy_moment { e } else { 1.0 };
        sum += f * if i == 0 || i == n {
            1.0
        } else if i % 2 == 0 {
            2.0
        } else {
            4.0
        };
    }
    sum * step / 3.0
}
fn angular(n: usize) -> Result<Vec<AngularCell>, ForwardError> {
    if ![8, 16, 32].contains(&n) {
        return Err(invalid());
    }
    let mut out = vec![
        AngularCell {
            mu: 0.0,
            count_fraction: 0.0
        };
        n
    ];
    for i in 0..n / 2 {
        let mut x = (std::f64::consts::PI * (i as f64 + 0.75) / (n as f64 + 0.5)).cos();
        let mut converged = false;
        for _ in 0..32 {
            let (p, pm) = legendre(n, x);
            let d = n as f64 * (x * p - pm) / (x * x - 1.0);
            let next = x - p / d;
            if (next - x).abs() <= 2.0 * f64::EPSILON {
                x = next;
                converged = true;
                break;
            }
            x = next;
        }
        if !converged {
            return Err(invalid());
        }
        let (p, pm) = legendre(n, x);
        let d = n as f64 * (x * p - pm) / (x * x - 1.0);
        let weight = positive(1.0 / ((1.0 - x * x) * d * d))?;
        out[i] = AngularCell {
            mu: -x,
            count_fraction: weight,
        };
        out[n - 1 - i] = AngularCell {
            mu: x,
            count_fraction: weight,
        };
    }
    Ok(out)
}
fn legendre(n: usize, x: f64) -> (f64, f64) {
    let (mut pm, mut p) = (1.0, x);
    for k in 2..=n {
        let q = ((2 * k - 1) as f64 * x * p - (k - 1) as f64 * pm) / k as f64;
        pm = p;
        p = q;
    }
    (p, pm)
}
impl AxbiSource {
    /// Allowed source sensitivity fesc is explicit; baseline .2, variant .25.
    /// nH0 is the a=1 comoving hydrogen density in cm^-3, not an endpoint proper
    /// density. Baseline cells_per_segment=16, n_mu=8; resolutions are numeric.
    pub fn new(
        n_h0_cm3: f64,
        escape_fraction: f64,
        cells_per_segment: usize,
        n_mu: usize,
    ) -> Result<Self, ForwardError> {
        positive(n_h0_cm3)?;
        positive(escape_fraction)?;
        if escape_fraction > 1.0 || !(1..=1024).contains(&cells_per_segment) {
            return Err(invalid());
        }
        let edges = [13.6_f64, 24.59, 54.42, 100.0];
        let mut integrals = Vec::new();
        for segment in edges.windows(2) {
            for j in 0..cells_per_segment {
                let lo = if j == 0 {
                    segment[0]
                } else {
                    segment[0] * (segment[1] / segment[0]).powf(j as f64 / cells_per_segment as f64)
                };
                let hi = if j + 1 == cells_per_segment {
                    segment[1]
                } else {
                    segment[0]
                        * (segment[1] / segment[0]).powf((j + 1) as f64 / cells_per_segment as f64)
                };
                let count = positive(simpson(lo, hi, 32, false))?;
                let energy = positive(simpson(lo, hi, 32, true))?;
                integrals.push((lo, hi, count, energy));
            }
        }
        let normalization = positive(integrals.iter().map(|x| x.2).sum())?;
        let reference = positive(simpson(edges[0], edges[3], 4096, false))?;
        let normalization_relative_residual = (normalization / reference - 1.0).abs();
        if normalization_relative_residual > 1e-7 {
            return Err(invalid());
        }
        let spectral_cells = integrals
            .into_iter()
            .map(|(lower_ev, upper_ev, count, energy)| SpectralCell {
                lower_ev,
                upper_ev,
                energy_ev: energy / count,
                photon_fraction: count / normalization,
            })
            .collect();
        let tail = positive(simpson(100.0, 500.0, 4096, false))?;
        Ok(Self {
            spectral_cells,
            angular_cells: angular(n_mu)?,
            normalization_relative_residual,
            omitted_blackbody_tail_photon_fraction: tail / (reference + tail),
            n_h0_cm3,
            escape_fraction,
        })
    }
    /// Continuous total emissivity. No year conversion is applied: MD14 psi is
    /// Msun/yr/cMpc^3 and the yield is photons/s per (Msun/yr).
    pub fn at_redshift(&self, zbar: f64) -> Result<EmissionRate, ForwardError> {
        if !zbar.is_finite() || !(4.0..=20.0).contains(&zbar) {
            return Err(invalid());
        }
        let zp = 1.0 + zbar;
        let sfr = 0.015 * zp.powf(2.7) / (1.0 + (zp / 2.9).powf(5.6));
        let log_yield = 1.0 / (0.00038 * 0.002_f64.powf(0.227) + 0.01858) - 2.55_f64.log10();
        let comoving = positive(self.escape_fraction * 10_f64.powf(log_yield) * sfr)?;
        let per_volume = positive(comoving / MPC_CM.powi(3))?;
        Ok(EmissionRate {
            comoving_photons_per_s_cmpc3: comoving,
            proper_photons_cm3_s: positive(per_volume * zp.powi(3))?,
            per_h_s: positive(per_volume / self.n_h0_cm3)?,
        })
    }
    /// Source-only derivative for one discrete photon population, H^-1 s^-1.
    /// Driver time integration and photon evolution remain separate owners.
    pub fn cell_per_h_s(
        &self,
        zbar: f64,
        energy_index: usize,
        angle_index: usize,
    ) -> Result<f64, ForwardError> {
        let e = self.spectral_cells.get(energy_index).ok_or_else(invalid)?;
        let a = self.angular_cells.get(angle_index).ok_or_else(invalid)?;
        positive(self.at_redshift(zbar)?.per_h_s * e.photon_fraction * a.count_fraction)
    }
}

AXBI_SOURCE_BOUNDARY
//! Campaign binding of the existing cold-through-reheated H/He point model.
//!
//! Grackle 3.4.1 literals are owned by `igm_rates`; event/energy ownership is
//! owned by `igm_point_rhs`. This adapter adds no coefficients or admission to
//! the FT03 interval model. The numerical operational domain does not assert
//! empirical fit accuracy, a continuum certificate, or a cosmological history.
use crate::{
    igm_rates::{igm_rates, IgmRates},
    igm_state::IgmGasState,
    igm_thermal::{igm_point_rhs, IgmPhotoInput, IgmPointRhs},
    ForwardError,
};

/// Identity shared by every point evaluation and any driver's point residual.
pub const AXBI_PROVIDER_ID: &str = "AXBI_GRACKLE_3_4_1_CASE_A_POINT_V1";
pub const GRACKLE_COMMIT: &str = "af7939494ce65007887ada7b98d1813df6843346";
pub const TEMPERATURE_DOMAIN_K: [f64; 2] = [1.0, 1.0e6];

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct AxbiPoint {
    pub provider_id: &'static str,
    /// Actual temperature recovered from the unchanged ideal H/He EOS.
    pub temperature_k: f64,
    pub rates: IgmRates,
    pub rhs: IgmPointRhs,
}

/// Case-A capture escape, clumping=1, primary-only externally supplied photo
/// input. Proper densities are cm^-3, expansion is s^-1, CMB temperature is K;
/// returned derivatives use proper seconds and the existing erg energy owners.
///
/// Scalar coefficients accept exactly [1,1e6] K. The point call admits its EOS
/// recovered temperature, preserving strict endpoint checks without projection.
/// No interval evaluator exists for this selected model. A solver residual must
/// call this same point path and must label its convergence evidence empirically.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct AxbiProvider;
impl AxbiProvider {
    pub fn rates(self, temperature_k: f64) -> Result<IgmRates, ForwardError> {
        igm_rates(temperature_k)
    }
    pub fn point(
        self,
        gas: &IgmGasState,
        n_h_cm3: f64,
        n_he_cm3: f64,
        mean_h_per_s: f64,
        tcmb_k: f64,
        photo: IgmPhotoInput,
    ) -> Result<AxbiPoint, ForwardError> {
        let temperature_k = gas.eos(n_h_cm3, n_he_cm3)?.temperature_k;
        let rhs = igm_point_rhs(gas, n_h_cm3, n_he_cm3, mean_h_per_s, tcmb_k, photo)?;
        let rates = self.rates(temperature_k)?;
        Ok(AxbiPoint {
            provider_id: AXBI_PROVIDER_ID,
            temperature_k,
            rates,
            rhs,
        })
    }
}

AXBI_SOURCE_BOUNDARY
//! Separate phenomenological low-temperature Grackle subset; no FT03 admission.
//!
//! Selected Case-A functions from Grackle 3.4.1, commit
//! af7939494ce65007887ada7b98d1813df6843346, rate_functions.c.
//! Copyright Enzo/Grackle development team; retained license and independent
//! literal-C reference are in tests/data/igm_grackle/. No call to the raw
//! provider is made, and its 100 K guard remains unchanged.
use crate::ForwardError;
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct IgmRateDiagnostics {
    /// Full coefficient of each floor-selected CI channel, not an excess correction.
    pub ci_floor_cm3_s: [f64; 3],
    /// Full coefficient of each cap-active CE channel; units follow ce_erg.
    pub ce_cap_erg: [f64; 3],
    pub dr_excluded_erg_cm3_s: f64,
    pub dr_low_branch: bool,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct IgmRates {
    pub ci_cm3_s: [f64; 3],
    pub rr_cm3_s: [f64; 3],
    pub dr_cm3_s: f64,
    /// HI, metastable HeI, HeII: erg cm3/s, erg cm6/s, erg cm3/s.
    /// Density products: ne*nHI, ne^2*nHeII, ne*nHeII respectively.
    pub ce_erg: [f64; 3],
    pub rr_cooling_erg_cm3_s: [f64; 3],
    pub dr_cooling_erg_cm3_s: f64,
    pub dr_raw_cooling_erg_cm3_s: f64,
    pub freefree_erg_cm3_s: f64,
    pub diagnostics: IgmRateDiagnostics,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
enum Process {
    K1,
    K2,
    K3,
    K5,
    K6,
    CeHI,
    CeHeI,
    CeHeII,
    ReHII,
    ReHeII1,
    ReHeII2,
    ReHeIII,
    Brem,
}
// Exact Grackle defaults, deliberately distinct from EOS CODATA kB.
const TINY: f64 = 1e-20;
const TEVK: f64 = 11605.0;
const KBOLTZ: f64 = 1.3806504e-16;
const LN_DHUGE: f64 = 69.07755278982137;
/// Operational package domain, not empirical support. No clamping of T.
/// Source literals: Grackle af7939494ce65007887ada7b98d1813df6843346.
/// CI floors and CE exponent caps are retained; DR cooling is disabled where
/// k4's DR reaction is disabled. Its excluded source residual remains visible.
pub fn igm_rates(t: f64) -> Result<IgmRates, ForwardError> {
    if !t.is_finite() || !(1.0..=1e6).contains(&t) {
        return Err(ForwardError::InvalidInput("IGM_TEMPERATURE_DOMAIN"));
    }
    use Process::*;
    let c = |p| source_coefficient(p, t);
    let ci = [c(K1)?, c(K3)?, c(K5)?];
    let te = t / TEVK;
    let low = te <= 0.8;
    let (rr_he, dr) = he_recombination(t);
    let ce = [c(CeHI)?, c(CeHeI)?, c(CeHeII)?];
    let dr_raw = c(ReHeII2)?;
    Ok(IgmRates {
        ci_cm3_s: ci,
        rr_cm3_s: [c(K2)?, rr_he, c(K6)?],
        dr_cm3_s: dr,
        ce_erg: ce,
        rr_cooling_erg_cm3_s: [c(ReHII)?, c(ReHeII1)?, c(ReHeIII)?],
        dr_cooling_erg_cm3_s: if low { 0.0 } else { dr_raw },
        dr_raw_cooling_erg_cm3_s: dr_raw,
        freefree_erg_cm3_s: c(Brem)?,
        diagnostics: IgmRateDiagnostics {
            ci_floor_cm3_s: std::array::from_fn(|i| if low && ci[i] == TINY { ci[i] } else { 0.0 }),
            ce_cap_erg: std::array::from_fn(|i| {
                if [118348.0, 13179.0, 473638.0][i] / t > LN_DHUGE {
                    ce[i]
                } else {
                    0.0
                }
            }),
            dr_excluded_erg_cm3_s: if low { dr_raw } else { 0.0 },
            dr_low_branch: low,
        },
    })
}
// Preserve the original C source literals, including excess decimal digits.
#[allow(clippy::excessive_precision)]
fn source_coefficient(process: Process, t: f64) -> Result<f64, ForwardError> {
    if !t.is_finite() || !(1.0..=1e6).contains(&t) {
        return Err(ForwardError::InvalidInput("IGM_TEMPERATURE_DOMAIN"));
    }
    use Process::*;
    let te = t / TEVK;
    let l = te.ln();
    let k1 = || {
        let v = (-32.71396786375 + 13.53655609057 * l - 5.739328757388 * l.powi(2)
            + 1.563154982022 * l.powi(3)
            - 0.2877056004391 * l.powi(4)
            + 0.03482559773736999 * l.powi(5)
            - 0.00263197617559 * l.powi(6)
            + 0.0001119543953861 * l.powi(7)
            - 2.039149852002e-6 * l.powi(8))
        .exp();
        if te <= 0.8 {
            v.max(TINY)
        } else {
            v
        }
    };
    let k3 = || {
        if te > 0.8 {
            (-44.09864886561001 + 23.91596563469 * l - 10.75323019821 * l.powi(2)
                + 3.058038757198 * l.powi(3)
                - 0.5685118909884001 * l.powi(4)
                + 0.06795391233790001 * l.powi(5)
                - 0.005009056101857001 * l.powi(6)
                + 0.0002067236157507 * l.powi(7)
                - 3.649161410833e-6 * l.powi(8))
            .exp()
        } else {
            TINY
        }
    };
    let k5 = || {
        if te > 0.8 {
            (-68.71040990212001 + 43.93347632635 * l - 18.48066993568 * l.powi(2)
                + 4.701626486759002 * l.powi(3)
                - 0.7692466334492 * l.powi(4)
                + 0.08113042097303 * l.powi(5)
                - 0.005324020628287001 * l.powi(6)
                + 0.0001975705312221 * l.powi(7)
                - 3.165581065665e-6 * l.powi(8))
            .exp()
        } else {
            TINY
        }
    };
    let v = match process {
        K1 => k1(),
        K2 => {
            if t > 5500.0 {
                (-28.61303380689232
                    - 0.7241125657826851 * l
                    - 0.02026044731984691 * l.powi(2)
                    - 0.002380861877349834 * l.powi(3)
                    - 0.0003212605213188796 * l.powi(4)
                    - 0.00001421502914054107 * l.powi(5)
                    + 4.989108920299513e-6 * l.powi(6)
                    + 5.755614137575758e-7 * l.powi(7)
                    - 1.856767039775261e-8 * l.powi(8)
                    - 3.071135243196595e-9 * l.powi(9))
                .exp()
            } else {
                he_recombination(t).0
            }
        }
        K3 => k3(),
        K5 => k5(),
        K6 => 3.36e-10 / t.sqrt() / (t / 1e3).powf(0.2) / (1.0 + (t / 1e6).powf(0.7)),
        CeHI => 7.5e-19 * (-(118348.0 / t).min(LN_DHUGE)).exp() / (1.0 + (t / 1e5).sqrt()),
        CeHeI => {
            9.1e-27 * (-(13179.0 / t).min(LN_DHUGE)).exp() * t.powf(-0.1687)
                / (1.0 + (t / 1e5).sqrt())
        }
        CeHeII => {
            5.54e-17 * (-(473638.0 / t).min(LN_DHUGE)).exp() * t.powf(-0.3970)
                / (1.0 + (t / 1e5).sqrt())
        }
        ReHII => {
            let lambda = 2.0 * 157807.0 / t;

            1.778e-29 * t * lambda.powf(1.965) / (1.0 + (lambda / 0.541).powf(0.502)).powf(2.697)
        }
        ReHeII1 => {
            let lambda = 2.0 * 285335.0 / t;

            3e-14 * KBOLTZ * t * lambda.powf(0.654)
        }
        ReHeII2 => {
            1.24e-13
                * t.powf(-1.5)
                * (-(470000.0 / t).min(LN_DHUGE)).exp()
                * (1.0 + 0.3 * (-(94000.0 / t).min(LN_DHUGE)).exp())
        }
        ReHeIII => {
            let lambda = 2.0 * 631515.0 / t;
            // Preserve original raw 8*T term; no kinetic-moment substitution.

            8.0 * 1.778e-29 * t * lambda.powf(1.965)
                / (1.0 + (lambda / 0.541).powf(0.502)).powf(2.697)
        }
        Brem => 1.43e-27 * t.sqrt() * (1.1 + 0.34 * (-(5.5 - t.log10()).powi(2) / 3.0).exp()),
    };
    if !v.is_finite() || v < 0.0 {
        return Err(ForwardError::InvalidInput("IGM_COEFFICIENT_INVALID"));
    }
    Ok(v)
}

// The two k4 summands, evaluated separately to avoid cancellation in tiny DR.
fn he_recombination(t: f64) -> (f64, f64) {
    let te = t / TEVK;
    let rr = 3.92e-13 / te.powf(0.6353);
    let dr = if te <= 0.8 {
        0.0
    } else {
        1.54e-9 * (1.0 + 0.3 / (8.099328789667 / te).exp())
            / ((40.49664394833662 / te).exp() * te.powf(1.5))
    };
    (rr, dr)
}

AXBI_SOURCE_BOUNDARY
//! Proper-time point chemistry and thermal ledger. No time integration.
//! Case A escape, clumping=1, primary-only externally specified photo rates.
//! Positive nonnormal input/intermediate arithmetic is rejected, not clipped.
//! The source CI floors/CE caps are the documented exception in igm_rates.
use crate::{igm_state::IgmGasState, ForwardError};
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct IgmPhotoInput {
    /// HI, HeI, HeII externally supplied ionizations per absorber per second.
    pub gamma_s: [f64; 3],
    /// HI, HeI, HeII heat power per absorber, NOT energy per event.
    /// Must vanish if the matching Gamma vanishes.
    pub heat_erg_per_absorber_s: [f64; 3],
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct IgmPointRhs {
    pub fraction_dt: [f64; 3],
    /// Chemical number-density derivatives: HI,HII,HeI,HeII,HeIII,e.
    pub species_chemical_cm3_s: [f64; 6],
    pub w_dt_erg_per_h_s: f64,
    pub temperature_dt_k_s: f64,
    pub photo_events_cm3_s: [f64; 3],
    pub ci_events_cm3_s: [f64; 3],
    pub rr_events_cm3_s: [f64; 3],
    pub dr_events_cm3_s: f64,
    pub ci_thermal_sink_erg_cm3_s: [f64; 3],
    pub rr_thermal_sink_erg_cm3_s: [f64; 3],
    pub dr_thermal_sink_erg_cm3_s: f64,
    pub ce_thermal_sink_erg_cm3_s: [f64; 3],
    pub freefree_thermal_sink_erg_cm3_s: f64,
    pub thermal_micro_erg_cm3_s: f64,
    pub binding_micro_erg_cm3_s: f64,
    pub escape_erg_cm3_s: f64,
    /// Signed INTO gas. CMB reservoir rate is its negative.
    pub cmb_to_gas_erg_cm3_s: f64,
    pub photo_input_erg_cm3_s: f64,
    pub expansion_work_erg_cm3_s: f64,
    pub ci_floor_events_cm3_s: [f64; 3],
    pub ce_cap_cooling_erg_cm3_s: [f64; 3],
    pub excluded_dr_cooling_erg_cm3_s: f64,
}

use crate::{igm_rates::igm_rates, HHeModel};
// CODATA 2022: https://physics.nist.gov/cuu/pdf/wall_2022.pdf
// sigma_T and electron mass; a_rad = 4*sigma_SB/c.
// sigma_SB follows the exact SI kB,h,c blackbody expression (converted to cgs).
const SIGMA_T_CM2: f64 = 6.6524587051e-25;
const ELECTRON_MASS_G: f64 = 9.1093837139e-28;
const PLANCK_ERG_S: f64 = 6.62607015e-27;
fn invalid() -> ForwardError {
    ForwardError::InvalidInput("IGM_RHS_DOMAIN_OR_ARITHMETIC")
}
fn finite(x: f64) -> Result<f64, ForwardError> {
    if x == 0.0 || x.is_normal() {
        Ok(x)
    } else {
        Err(invalid())
    }
}
fn nonnegative(x: f64) -> Result<f64, ForwardError> {
    finite(x)?;
    if x >= 0.0 {
        Ok(x)
    } else {
        Err(invalid())
    }
}
fn mul(xs: &[f64]) -> Result<f64, ForwardError> {
    for &x in xs {
        nonnegative(x)?;
    }
    if xs.contains(&0.0) {
        return Ok(0.0);
    }
    let mut y = 1.0;
    for &x in xs {
        y *= x;
        if !y.is_normal() {
            return Err(invalid());
        }
    }
    Ok(y)
}
fn divide(x: f64, y: f64) -> Result<f64, ForwardError> {
    finite(x)?;
    if !y.is_normal() || y <= 0.0 {
        return Err(invalid());
    }
    let v = x / y;
    finite(v)?;
    if x != 0.0 && v == 0.0 {
        return Err(invalid());
    }
    Ok(v)
}
/// Immutable point evaluator. Errors return no partially updated state.
/// Admission uses the recovered EOS temperature strictly, not an originally
/// requested constructor temperature. Even sub-ulp endpoint reconstruction
/// outside 1..=1e6 K is rejected; no state/evaluation projection is performed.
/// `h` is nonnegative expansion s^-1; no coordinate conversion is hidden here.
/// Fractions for an absent helium element have exactly zero derivatives.
pub fn igm_point_rhs(
    state: &IgmGasState,
    nh: f64,
    nhe: f64,
    h: f64,
    tcmb: f64,
    photo: IgmPhotoInput,
) -> Result<IgmPointRhs, ForwardError> {
    nonnegative(h)?;
    if !tcmb.is_normal() || tcmb <= 0.0 {
        return Err(invalid());
    }
    for i in 0..3 {
        nonnegative(photo.gamma_s[i])?;
        nonnegative(photo.heat_erg_per_absorber_s[i])?;
        if photo.gamma_s[i] == 0.0 && photo.heat_erg_per_absorber_s[i] != 0.0 {
            return Err(invalid());
        }
    }
    let eos = state.eos(nh, nhe)?;
    let t = eos.temperature_k;
    let ne = eos.electron_density_cm3;
    let rates = igm_rates(t)?;
    let c = HHeModel::controlled_fixture();
    let [x, y, z] = state.fractions;
    let abs = [
        mul(&[nh, 1.0 - x])?,
        mul(&[nhe, 1.0 - (y + z)])?,
        mul(&[nhe, y])?,
    ];
    let ions = [mul(&[nh, x])?, mul(&[nhe, y])?, mul(&[nhe, z])?];
    let mut out = IgmPointRhs::default();
    let mut photo_heat = 0.0;
    let mut photo_bind = 0.0;
    let mut capture_bind = 0.0;
    for i in 0..3 {
        let chi = mul(&[c.threshold_ev[i], c.ev_erg])?;
        out.photo_events_cm3_s[i] = mul(&[abs[i], photo.gamma_s[i]])?;
        out.ci_events_cm3_s[i] = mul(&[ne, abs[i], rates.ci_cm3_s[i]])?;
        out.rr_events_cm3_s[i] = mul(&[ne, ions[i], rates.rr_cm3_s[i]])?;
        out.ci_thermal_sink_erg_cm3_s[i] = mul(&[chi, out.ci_events_cm3_s[i]])?;
        out.rr_thermal_sink_erg_cm3_s[i] = mul(&[ne, ions[i], rates.rr_cooling_erg_cm3_s[i]])?;
        out.ci_floor_events_cm3_s[i] = mul(&[ne, abs[i], rates.diagnostics.ci_floor_cm3_s[i]])?;
        photo_heat += mul(&[abs[i], photo.heat_erg_per_absorber_s[i]])?;
